Post-episode Reflexion (not sent to GM):
New plan: My previous attempts failed because I fell into an infinite loop of traveling between rooms (Kitchen, Pantry, Magic Workshop) to manage inventory, never actually executing the brewing steps on the cauldron. I also failed to account for the gravity mechanic in the Magic Workshop, which caused me to drop the fire wand on the floor where it became inaccessible or difficult to retrieve without specific actions, and I neglected to collect the ectoplasm from the Cellar early enough.

My new strategy is to minimize travel by fetching all necessary items in a single efficient route before returning to the kitchen to brew, and to handle the gravity mechanic proactively.

1. **Gather Phase (Single Route):**
* Go to **Storage Room**: Take `borpulus`, `plonkle crystal`, and `plunkle crystal`. (Drop `potion recipe` if inventory is full, as I know the steps).
* Go to **Cellar** (from Storage Room): Take `bucket of ectoplasm`.
* Go to **Pantry** (via Hallway/Reading Room/Garden/Storage Room path): Take `peach` from the ice box.
* Go to **Magic Workshop**: Immediately take the `fire wand` while floating. Do not drop it. If inventory is full, drop the `potion recipe` (if still held) or a non-essential item, but keep the wand.
* *Crucial*: If I cannot hold all items, I will drop items on the **Kitchen floor** (a safe zone with normal gravity) rather than the Magic Workshop floor, then retrieve them one by one to brew.

2. **Brewing Phase (In Kitchen):**
* Ensure I am in the **Kitchen**.
* **Step 1**: `pour bucket of ectoplasm into cauldron`.
* **Step 2**: `mix cauldron with borpulus`.
* **Step 3**: `add plonkle crystal to cauldron`.
* **Step 4**: `add peach to cauldron`.
* **Step 5**: `swirl fire wand at cauldron`.
* **Step 6**: `add plunkle crystal to cauldron`.

3. **Completion**:
* Once the final step is acknowledged, immediately type `> done`.

I will strictly avoid the "go to X, take item, go to Y, drop item" loop. If I need to swap items, I will do it in the Kitchen or a single room, then proceed immediately to the next recipe step. I will not leave the Kitchen until the potion is brewed.
